Covers ICML 2026, ICLR 2026, NeurIPS 2025, AAAI 2026, CVPR 2026, KDD 2026, ACL 2026, EMNLP 2025, SIGIR 2026, WWW 2026, CIKM 2025, RecSys 2025. Key awards: ICML Outstanding = Flexibility Trap + High-Accuracy Diffusion Sampling; NeurIPS Best = Artificial Hivemind + Gated Attention (Qwen/Alibaba) + 1000-Layer RL + Why Diffusion Don't Memorize; CVPR Best = D4RT (Google DeepMind 4D reconstruction); ACL Best = Imperfective Paradox (NII/Tokyo); RecSys Best = Conformal Risk Control (EC JRC); ICLR Outstanding = Transformers Succinct + LLMs Lost in Multi-Turn; WWW Best = MedRGAG. Industry highlights: Netflix GenRec LLM-backed ranker, Meta ConnectionMind social-graph rec, Alibaba MetaStrategy LLM-executable ranking, BMW CAR-bench, NVIDIA NitroGen gaming. Cross-conference themes: diffusion as first-class (ICML+NeurIPS+CVPR), RL post-training (RLVR/GRPO debate), LLM-as-ranker, agent safety, 4D reconstruction. | 12 venues + general arXiv (Jul–Aug 2026) |

PCG: scheduled-inpainting interactive generative motion editing (Disney Research/ETH tentative) [2607.29133]. Benchmarks: none new. Industry: The AI Wave & Reinvention of Game Discovery — Steam oversupply (Gini 0.96, top 1% = 73.5% play hours), agentic player-game matching [2607.25010]; AI as a Democratizing Force in Indie Game Development — 14-month agentic production-platform log, planning $0.27–0.58/plan [2608.07825]. Related: Generalised Reachability Games PSPACE-completeness + FPT + memory bounds [2607.14199]; Generative Modeling as Mean-Field Game Design (MFGLab) [2607.23026]; Draining the Energy Commons — LLM-collective over-appropriation coordination failure (Sapienza tentative) [2607.22188]. Trend: world models chase action-responsiveness not just fidelity; chess as reasoning-alignment stress test; cooperative MARL theory matures |
